## ORCHESTRATOR ERRATUM AT PEER-ROUND CLOSE 2026-08-31 — E-21-instance (new ORCHESTRATOR vector), self-caught same turn, fully cured
- WHAT HAPPENED: the batch-C durable-mirror loop built its path pairs with the PowerShell format operator inside array literals ("...{0:d2}..." -f $n, "..." -f $n collapses to ONE element by operator precedence), so Copy-Item received NULL destinations: five r2 packets were copied as DUPLICATES into the shared SP\Jer ROOT (E-21-class stray writes, orchestrator vector) and the five durable mirrors were NEVER written. Compounding: the loop's hash check threw on the null paths and fail-OPENED — the summary line printed a FALSE "mirrors byte-identical: 9 files" while only 4 pairs were actually verified, with five Get-FileHash error lines standing right beside it.
- DETECTION: orchestrator read of the command's own error stream against its green summary (the E-03 root-cause pattern — never trust a green line beside visible errors); targeted inspection then confirmed both arms (5 root strays; durable=False x5).
- CURE (verified): each stray hash-compared against its reviews\ original — all five byte-identical — then removed; the five packets re-mirrored durable with per-file hash verification (5/5 verified); _sp_stray_sweep re-run CLEAN (210 files); no other file touched. Ledger: E-21 supersede-with-note appended (MD + JSONL) carrying the two forward behaviors — no format-operator pair construction in array literals; orchestrator verification loops fail CLOSED when a comparison can throw.
- IMPACT: zero — no packet content changed anywhere (strays were byte-identical duplicates); durable state is now complete and hash-verified; the sweep gate + error-stream reading caught it within the same close sequence. Counterfactual honestly scored: without the error-stream read, the batch-C packets would have been ABSENT from durable while CYCLE_STATE claimed them mirrored — the next cold resume's SP rebuild would have missed five packets and the docket's packet_sha256 would have flagged it at consolidation re-verify; the durability layer had a second net.

PERMANENT SAFEGUARDS (every generated prompt carries these until the marathon completes at 66/66): no M7 or comparison-data reads ever; translation punctuation, editorial headings, and other tier-4 metadata never drive a boundary (E-23); corpus-wide-order execution parity - every CWO executes as its OWN sweep, never folded per-row (E-18); the hard book-close gates of CAMPAIGN_CLOSE_GATE.v1.md bind every book close; a fresh full second-generation sweep runs after each repair wave with a checker distinct from the repair author (_wave_repair_sweep.py is the staged deterministic arm); attempt receipts record actual model/effort, exact denominators, producer/catcher roles, evidence paths, findings, and hashes; Anthropic-family agreement is one correlated M8 voice, never independent confirmation; Revelation requires Opus 5 high review plus Fable 5 high adversarial adjudication on the six named seam classes (vision-cycle, speaker/voice, quotation, recapitulation-versus-sequence, textual-variant, cross-chapter); audit completion is distinct from repair completion: a DONE audit receipt never implies repairs, the remediation docket's open/resolved dispositions and independent post-repair evidence are tracked, and low findings are retained with explicit handling; every repair proposal is validated as adversarially as the row it repairs: replacement claims need independent source evidence or qualification, unexpanded placeholders are rejected by every lane verifier, and proposals are resolved from source bytes before any application; seams are assessed from both sides under the book's governing rules (refrains close units): an onset-only diagnosis is incomplete until the adjacent unit's close or onset is weighed from bytes; when a pinned shared tool changes, a dependency-applicability record preserves the old evidence, records old/new hashes and the scope of change, and states whether earlier receipts are affected; history is never silently rewritten and unchanged work is never reflexively redone; model reporting stays honest: the actual model is evidenced; high ordered is not high verified when the runtime exposes no effective-effort evidence; the pre-feedback baseline is preserved and every subsequent repair or re-adjudication is labeled; external quality-control observations are never convergence evidence or a segmentation vote — every check re-derives from the M8 raw witnesses and the owner-ruled strategy; the pre-print checker verifies canonical prohibitions, rejects contradictory instructions, and binds the prompt to the latest durable cursor, attempt dispositions and audit-versus-repair state; completed one-time checks stay completed and are never rerun merely after a clear.
OPERATIONAL LAWS (pointers - the ledger is the carrier): the E-13 research-context preamble PLUS both E-19 lines (the affirmative no-check line and the exact-path law line) go in EVERY launch message (the preamble text is durable in the briefs' headers and in CYCLE_STATE's 2026-09-03 staging census); the E-14 deliverable ladder governs kills (ABSENT resume-in-place same attempt id; PRESENT validate never re-run); E-22 governs HTTP-429 spend-limit kills (stop launching, record state, re-verify governance on reset, then the E-14 ladder per agent — the 2026-09-03 W1 kill of 10/10 resumed in place 10/10); census numbers come from the tool's COUNT, never from prose arithmetic; every lane verifier accepts grounds as a string or a list of strings, reports any other type, and rejects unexpanded placeholders; the owner budget law stands (soft cap ~7M subagent tokens/session, MANDATORY owner check-in before crossing 8M; overrun OK when necessary, the bar is no WASTED tokens; Jer reported spend ~36.9M of the ruled ~45M +/-20% plus the recorded E-22 and a04 reporting gaps).
AT SESSION CLOSE (or any boundary): append the batch census + cursor to SP/Jer/freeze/CYCLE_STATE.md; record any NEW owner directive received during the session VERBATIM in the durable carriers (ERROR_PATTERN_LEDGER.v1.md + error_pattern_ledger.v1.jsonl, CAMPAIGN_CLOSE_GATE.v1.md, and CYCLE_STATE) BEFORE closing, so every owner instruction survives every context clear; mirror the changed durable set with per-file hash verification; then GENERATE the next session's resume prompt fresh from the latest durable cursor, receipts and STATE-BINDING (python SP\Jer\_safe_to_clear_check.py --binding; never carry consumed batch instructions or completed rechecks as active work), verify it MECHANICALLY with SP\Jer\_safe_to_clear_check.py (v2: it must print PASS before the prompt is printed — canonical clauses, contradiction rejection, state binding, receipt + docket pointers with current hashes), WRITE the verified prompt to C:\wt\logos-t423-m8-fable\.ai\scratch\multi_model_bible_chunking\M8_fable\RESUME_PROMPT_CURRENT.md (overwrite - it is the single current-prompt slot; a fresh session that starts WITHOUT a pasted prompt reads and executes that file, found via the persistent memory pointer), and PRINT the verified prompt to the owner as the session's final message, explicitly telling the owner it is the prompt to paste after clearing context - always including this same record-generate-verify-write-print instruction so every session ends by handing the owner the prompt that starts the next one.

## E-24 NEAR-MISS + CORRECTIVE CONTROLS 2026-09-06 (OW-2 backlog repair lane; zero shipped impact)
- WHAT: NEAR-MISS 2026-09-06, zero shipped impact: the Job apply would have written 10 touched + 97 shifted rows with stale content addresses into book_chunks/Job/chunks.jsonl and whole_bible_chunk_map.jsonl (reversible via the write-once pre-feedback baseline; detectable by recomputation); caught after the deterministic sweep PASSED and before apply - the deterministic pass was not semantic verification, exactly as the ruling warns. Song (already applied) carries no final_sha256 - unaffected, verified by recomputation audit. Jer author waves: Jer rows carry no final_sha256 (Isa close precedent) - unaffected
- ROOT CAUSE: a per-row DEPENDENT field with an undocumented recipe (final_sha256 = sha256 of the row minus that field, JSON ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True; carried only by the Job, Neh and Esth shipped rows) rode through the repair pipeline as an inert copy: the packet row carried the shipped hash forward after its content changed, and the sweep's chunk_index renumber (a retire shifts every later index) changed the content of 97 untouched Job rows without any recomputation; nothing in the sweep, the apply, the briefs or the schema notes named the recipe
- CONTROLS INSTALLED (SP/Jer tools; mirrored at close): sweep: recompute final_sha256 for EVERY sim row whose content differs from its shipped row (touched by a packet OR shifted by the renumber), fail on any non-inherited incoherence, record inherited incoherence (row byte-identical to shipped with an already-incoherent shipped hash) without touching it; semcheck slices carry the row AS IT WILL SHIP (repaired_row_as_shipped) with a pipeline-owned-fields note and SEMCHECK_BRIEF names the two pipeline-owned fields; the r2 order builder never orders a pipeline-owned field and routes a cross-row / partner-row finding to the row it names; plan authorization in the sweep is judged per cycle (a later orders file no longer overrides the earlier plan for the same row - the second causal root found by the same regression run)
- REGRESSION: 2026-09-06 recomputation audit over every shipped book + the whole-bible map: Job 194/194 coherent (pre-apply); Neh 88/88 and Esth 43/43 INCOHERENT - pre-existing, campaign-inherited, NOT this lane's change, left untouched and reported to the owner as an observation; no other shipped book carries the field (Ps omitted it honestly at its build). Regression: Job sweep re-run over the r1 packets -> recomputed 10 touched + 97 shifted, inherited 0, independent recheck 0 incoherent
- SIBLINGS: Song (applied) and Jer (in progress) carry no final_sha256 - unaffected; Neh 88/88 + Esth 43/43 pre-existing incoherent hashes = owner observation, untouched.
- SECOND CYCLE STATE: Job r2 (r2a: 8 rows incl. partner M8-Job-124; r2b: 4 rows incl. partners M8-Job-169/180/182), Ps r2 (r2a: 8 rows incl. partners M8-Ps-168/172; r2b: 8 rows incl. partners M8-Ps-431/464/491), Eccl r2 packet landed (5 rows) and its targeted semcheck sc_Eccl_r201 launched; findings on pipeline-owned fields recorded, not ordered.
